Let's design a more effective optimizer than previous. Key ideas:

Implement `kkt_refine(u0, n, deadline, rng, beta, inner_iters, nu)`:
  u=normalize_u(u0,n); S=fft_size; best.
  loop while time:
    b=conv(u); G,beta weights. G smooth max; r=G/n.
    For inner in 1..inner:
       y variable; FISTA on Q with fixed H/r.
       Start z = u; maybe z=normalize u.
       For iter in range(K):
          h = H(z) rfft? v = h - r
          hv = H(v)
          grad = 4*hv + 2*nu*(z - u0)  # derivative of ||H z - r||² + ν||z-u0||²? derivative ν*2(z-u0)
          g = grad - mean(grad) ? For equality sum, subtract mean from gradient before projection (Projected gradient method for convex with simplex: we can add constant mean; since sum constraint, constant on simplex same; subtract mean for numerical.
          step = 1/(4 + 2*nu) (since L=4(1+ν)? Actually gradient =4H²z +2νz + const, Lipschitz 4||H||² +2ν =4+2ν). Use safe α=1/L maybe 0.5/L.
          q = project(z - α*g, n)
          accept (convex, no line search)
          update y/t
       u=z
    u=normalize/sym; update best.
    # maybe every outer, explicit smooth max gradient step to reduce max.

This uses 2 H mult per inner iter + line projection (binary search O(1)). For n=8192, H mult one rfft+irfft maybe ~1ms, two ~2ms; 1000 inner ~2s. Good.

Data:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p <<=1
    return p
```

`fft_plan(n)` maybe returns S_conv = next_pow2(2n-1), S_h = next_pow2(3n-2). We can just use S = next_pow2(3n-2) for both; conv uses first 2n-1. For n=16384, S=65536 (3n=49150 -> 65536). rfft length S/2+1. Memory ~65536 complex ~1MB, fine. Use one S.

`def conv_u(u, S)`:
```
F = np.fft.rfft(u, S)
b = np.fft.irfft(F*F, S)[:2*n-1]
# avoid tiny neg
# b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
return b, F
```
Need n global? Pass Lc=2*n-1.

`def smooth_w(b, beta)`:
```
m=np.max(b)
if beta<=0: w uniform
x=beta*(b-m)
x=x- max? 0 by m, but if m inf no.
e=np.exp(np.clip(x, -745, 0))
z=e.sum()
if z<=0: w one at argmax
w=e/z
G=m + np.log(z)/beta
return G,w
```
In u scale, G ~ n; if beta 0.5, logZ/beta maybe O(1), ok.

`def hankel_y(u, w, S)`:
Use n, wc=len(w)=2n-1. Need size S_h = next_pow2(n+wc-1)=next_pow2(3n-2). Use same S. Then:
```
Fu = np.fft.rfft(u, S)
Fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], S)
C = np.fft.irfft(Fu*Fw, S)[:n+wc-1]
h = C[wc-n:wc][::-1]
return h
```
Check slice for m=wc: m-n..m-1 length n. For n=1, wc=1, m-n=0; C[0:1][::-1] ok.
Potential memory: w[::-1] view. Good.

Can we reuse Fu from conv for hankel? In smooth FISTA, we need w from b computed from F_u. Compute Fu once, b, w, then h using Fu. Good.

`def smooth_objective_grad_u(u, S, beta, return_conv=False)`:

def smooth_fista(u0,n,S,deadline,rng,beta_schedule=(0.5,2,10,50)):

Inner smooth:
```

for beta beta:
  for iter:
    b, w, G, h, g = objective_grad(y,beta)
    grad = g - mean; gnorm=norm; if small break

    # step length scale? Use base maybe 0.5; to adapt base by beta: base = min(2, base*1.05)
    accepted=False

`block_to_uv(n,B,c)`:
```
L=n//B
if B==1: return np.ones(n)* (n/1)?? uniform u=1
tmp=np.empty(B); tmp[:-1]=c; tmp[-1]=0
tmp = tmp - max(tmp) (if finite) else zeros
w=np.exp(tmp); s=w.sum(); if bad uniform; w/=s

return u
```

def kkt_inner(u,n,S,deadline,beta,nu.inner, max_outer=None):
   last_time=time
   while time < deadline:
      # compute b G w
      Fu=rfft(u,S); b=...; G,w=smooth_w(b,beta)
      score=2*max(b)/(sum^2/n?) compute; update best
      r=G/n
      Fw=rfft(w[::-1],S)
      # Q FISTA
      z=normalize_u(u,n)
      y=z.copy(); t=1.0; alpha=1.0/(4+2*nu)
      for _ in range(inner_iters):
         if time>deadline: break
         h = hankel_pre(z,Fw,S,n)
         v = h - r
         hv = hankel_pre(v,Fw,S,n)
         grad = 4*hv + 2*nu*(z - u)
         grad -= mean(grad)

         q=project(z - alpha*grad, n)
         # FISTA update
         rho=(t-1)/(t+1)
         y = q + rho*(q - z)
         y = normalize_u(y,n)
         z = q
         t = 0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))

         eps=1e-12
         ratio = r / (h + eps)

Let's implement active set exact gradient line search as a lightweight final step:
```
def active_gradient_refine(u,n,S,deadline,rng):
  while time:
    b=conv(u); maxb=max(b); tol=max(1e-6, 1e-3*maxb)
    mask = b >= maxb - tol
    w = mask/mask.sum()
    h=hankel(u,w)
    g=2*h - mean
    gnorm=norm(g)
    if gnorm <1e-12: break
    d=g/gnorm
    best_trial=None
    for alpha in [1,2,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,...]:
       q=project(u - alpha*d,n)
       bq=conv(q); Mq=max(bq)
       if Mq < maxb: accept, break
    if accepted: u=q; if no improvement after X: perturb
```

def trig_start(n,rng,base_uniform=True):
    x=np.zeros(n)
    for mode in [1,2,3,4,5]:
       amp = rng.uniform(0.2,1.0) / mode
       if mode==1: # asymmetric may help
           x += amp*rng.normal(size=n)
    # lowpass: rfft z random power lowfreq
    # simpler: z=zeros; zc=rfft(z?); no just random smooth via ifft of random low freq.

Spectral random:
```
def spectral_noise_start(n,rng,cutoff=8,amp=1.0):
   zc = rng.normal(size=n//2+1) +1j*rng.normal(...)
   for k>cutoff and k<n-cutoff: zc[k]=0 (and real symmetry)
   z = real(ifft(zc))[:n]
   u = 1 + amp*np.tanh(z)
   u = np.maximum(u,1e-6); normalize
```
This yields smooth shape. Use multiple amps 0.3,0.7,1.2.

Detailed functions:

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p<<=1
    return p
```

`fft_size_for_n(n)`: if n<=1 return 1? But need 3n-2. For n=1, 3n-2=1, S=1. rfft size 1 okay? np.fft.rfft length 1. hankel need 1. We'll mostly n≥8.

def make_fft_size(n): return _next_pow2(max(1, 3*n-2))

`normalize_u(v,target,sym=False)`:
```
v=np.asarray(v,float)
if v.size==0: return np.full(target,int)? no target is value not size. Actually target sum. If v size=0 return None? For start size known.
v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),v,0.0)
v=np.maximum(v,0.0)
if sym and v.size>1: v=0.5*(v+v[::-1])
s=v.sum()
if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones(v.size,dtype=float)*target/v.size
return v*(target/s)

m=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0

```

```
def hankel_from_Fu(Fu,w,S,n):
   wc=w.size; need=n+wc-1
   Fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],S)
   C=np.fft.irfft(Fu*Fw,S)[:need]
   return C[wc-n:wc][::-1]
```
Then smooth_grad uses Fu.

def multiplicative_step(u,w,r,n,S,rho=0.5):
   h=hankel(u,w,S)

   factor = rho*(r/denom) + (1-rho)

   return u2
```

Implementation details:
```
def block_search(n,S,deadline,rng,max_B=1024,prev_u=None,verbose=True):
    best_u=np.ones(n); best_sc=score_u(best_u,S)
    B_list=[]
    for B in (8,16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048,4096):
        if B<=max_B and B<n and n%B==0: B_list.append(B)
    if not B_list: return best,best_sc
    # time per B: maybe more for larger? Equal. Use while time.
    for idx,B in enumerate(B_list):
       if time>=deadline: break
       alloc = (deadline - t_start)/(len(B_list)-idx) *0.9
       sub = min(deadline, time+max(20, alloc))
       best_u, best_sc = block_refine_one_B(n,S,sub,rng,B,best_u,prev_u)
       # after one B, use best as prev for next? We can pass prev_u=best_u.
       prev_u=best_u
    return best_u,best_sc
```
`block_refine_one_B`:
- B small, starts list.
- Run L-BFGS beta=1 then beta=4 needed? To save time, for each init:
  - if B <=64: starts incl 8; beta runs [0.5, 2]? For each start maybe too many. Use for each start one run with beta=1. Then best and one beta=4.
- `bounds=[(-8,8)]*(B-1)`
- Need `c0` shape B-1.
- After each run, convert to u, score; update local best.
- At end, if time, run final beta=8 from best.
- Return.

But if B large and many starts, time could exceed sub. We check.

`block_objective` uses `smooth_grad_u`; objective returns `G` (smooth max of uu) not exact score. L-BFGS may minimize G. At beta=1, G approx max. At beta=8, sharper. Good.

`c_from_u` function:
```
def c_from_block_u(u,n,B):
   L=n//B
   h=np.maximum(u.reshape(B,L).mean(axis=1),1e-8)
   c=np.log(h[:-1]/h[-1])
   return np.clip(c,-12,12)
```

```
def top_k_candidates(candidates,k):
   res=[]
   for cand in candidates:
      for i in range(len(res)):
         if cand[2] < res[i][2]:
             # insert
             res.insert(i,cand); break
      else:
         if len(res)<k: res.append(cand)
      if len(res)>k: res.pop()
   return res
```

def u_to_seq(u):
   u=np.asarray(u,float)
   u=np.where(np.isfinite(u),u,0.0)
   u=np.clip(u,0.0,1000.0)
   return [float(x) for x in u]
```

```
def active_kkt_refine(u0,n,S,deadline,rng,sym=False,plans=None,verbose_label=''):
    if plans is None: plans = DEFAULT_ACTIVE_PLANS
    u=normalize_u(u0,n,sym)
    best_u=u.copy(); best_sc=score_u(u,S)
    no_improve=0
    t0=time.monotonic()
    nplans=len(plans)
    for idx, plan in enumerate(plans):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       frac, nu, inner = plan
       # remaining subdeadline equal share:
       rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
       sub_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, rem / (nplans - idx) * 0.95)
       if sub_deadline > deadline: sub_deadline=deadline
       Lc=2*n-1
       # inner while time < sub_deadline
       while time.monotonic() < sub_deadline:
           b,Fu=convolution_u(u,S)
           sc=score_from_b(b,n,float(np.sum(u)))
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
              best_sc=sc; best_u=u.copy(); no_improve=0
           else:
              no_improve += 1
           if no_improve > 60:
              # perturb
              if rng.random() < 0.5:
                 u=normalize_u(u*(1.0+0.05*rng.normal(size=n)),n,sym)
              else:
                 u=normalize_u(u + 0.02*rng.normal(size=n), n, sym)
              no_improve=0
           maxb=float(np.max(b))
           K=int(round(frac*Lc))
           if K<1: K=1
           if K>Lc: K=Lc
           if K==Lc:
              idxs=np.arange(Lc)
           else:
              idxs=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
           w=np.zeros(Lc,dtype=float)
           w[idxs]=1.0/float(K)
           weighted=float(np.sum(b[idxs])/K)
           r=weighted/float(n)
           if not finite or r<=0: r=1.0?
           Fw=np.fft.rfft(np.ascontiguousarray(w[::-1]), S)
           # Q FISTA
           z=normalize_u(u,n,sym)
           y=z.copy()
           t=1.0
           Lstep=1.0/(2.0*(1.0+nu))
           for _ in range(inner):
               if time.monotonic() >= sub_deadline: break
               h=hankel_pre(z,Fw,S,n)
               v=h-r
               hv=hankel_pre(v,Fw,S,n)
               grad=2.0*hv + 2.0*nu*(z-u)
               grad=grad - float(np.mean(grad))
               # if grad norm too large? line? use alpha; but to avoid overflow:
               gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
               if not finite or gnorm < 1e-14:
                   break
               # maybe cap effective step length
               # If gnorm > 10, reduce alpha_eff = alpha*10/gnorm? Since gradient scale may large if bad u. Use alpha_eff = min(alpha, 1.0/gnorm?) Hmm.
               # Projected gradient with fixed step L safe if L bound holds; gnorm can be large but step = alpha*grad; if grad huge (u extreme), step huge causing projection to uniform. Maybe okay. To be safe, cap L2 step to 2*sqrt(n)? Actually step norm = alpha*gnorm. For n=8192, sqrt n=90. If gnorm 1000, step 450 huge. The L bound should account; large gradient means far from optimum, projection may snap. Could cap `alpha_eff = alpha` and if alpha*gnorm > 2*sqrt(n): alpha_eff = 2*sqrt(n)/gnorm`.
               step_norm = Lstep*gnorm
               if step_norm > 2.0*float(np.sqrt(n)):
                   alpha_eff = 2.0*float(np.sqrt(n))/gnorm
               else:
                   alpha_eff = Lstep
               q=project_simplex(z - alpha_eff*grad, n)
               if sym: q=normalize_u(q,n,sym)
               # FISTA update
               rho=(t-1.0)/(t+1.0)
               y = q + rho*(q - z)
               y = normalize_u(y,n,sym)
               z = q
               t = 0.5*(1.0+float(np.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t)))
           u=normalize_u(z,n,sym)
           # check score of u maybe if improved best
           b2,F2=convolution_u(u,S)
           sc2=score_from_b(b2,n,float(np.sum(u)))
           if sc2 < best_sc:
              best_sc=sc2; best_u=u.copy(); no_improve=0
       u=best_u if not np.isfinite? Should we set u=best_u to avoid diverging? We intentionally allow exploration but if score worsens too much, maybe reset to best? We can if current score > 2.2 (worse than uniform) reset? Score max maybe. It could diverge to score large. Let's maintain current u as z for exploration, but if score > 4.0 reset to best_u? Score upper bound could be 2n (all mass one) huge. To avoid bad, after each outer if sc > 2.5 maybe set u=best_u and perturb? But exploration from bad might recover. `convolution_u(u,S)` should return `b` and `Fu`. If `Fp` not needed? Use.
```
def convolution_u(u,S):
    n=u.size; Lc=2*n-1
    Fu=np.fft.rfft(u,S)
    b=np.fft.irfft(Fu*Fu,S)[:Lc]
    return b,Fu
```
Could `Fu` length S/2+1. Good.

`hankel_pre` expects Fw. Need Fw for w length Lc. `S` same. For `h=H(z)` with z length n, need size 3n-2. S from fft_size. Good.

Now `smooth_grad_u`:
```
def smooth_grad_u(u,S,beta):
    b,Fu=convolution_u(u,S)
    G,w=smooth_max_weights(b,beta)
    h=hankel_from_Fu(Fu,w,S,u.size)
    g=2*h
    g=g-float(np.mean(g))
    return G,g,b

def run_block_minimize(c0,n,B,beta,bounds,S):
   c0=np.clip(np.asarray(c0,float).reshape(-1), -12, 12)
   if not HAVE_SCIPY: return c0
   options={'maxiter':60,'ftol':1e-9,'gtol':1e-5,'maxls':30,'maxcor':10}
   try: res=minimize(block_objective,c0,args=(n,B,beta,S),method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=bounds,options=options)

   if not finite: c=c0
   return np.clip(c,-12,12)

def block_search(n,S,deadline,rng,max_B,prev_u=None):
   best_u=np.ones(n); best_sc=score_u(best_u,S)
   B_list=[]...
   t0=time.monotonic()
   for idx,B in enumerate(B_list):
       if time>=deadline: break
       rem=deadline-time
       # allocate at least maybe rem/(remaining)
       sub=min(deadline,time+max(20, rem/(len(B_list)-idx)*0.9))
       best_u,best_sc=block_search_one_B(n,S,sub,rng,B,best_sc,best_u,prev_u)
       prev_u=best_u
   return best_u,best_sc
```

def block_initial_list(B,rng,prev_u=None,n=None):
   inits=[]
   if B>1:
     m=B-1
     inits.append(np.zeros(m))
     x=np.linspace(0,1,m)
     for a in (-4,4,-8,8): inits.append(a*(x-0.5))
     half=m//2; c=zeros; c[:half]=3; c[half:] = -3; inits.append(c)
     t=np.linspace(0,2*np.pi,m,endpoint=False)
     for mode,a in ((1,2),(2,2),(3,1.5)):
       inits.append(a*np.cos(mode*t)); inits.append(a*np.sin(mode*t))
     inits.append(1.0*rng.normal(0,1,m)); inits.append(1.5*rng.normal(0,1,m))
   else: inits.append(np.empty(0))
   if prev_u is not None and n is not None and n% B==0 and B>1:
       c=c_from_block_u(prev_u,n,B); inits.insert(0,c)
   # clip
   return [np.clip(np.asarray(c,float).reshape(-1),-10,10) for c in inits]
```

def spectral_u(n,rng,amp=0.8,cutoff=8):
   zc=rng.normal(size=n//2+1)+1j*rng.normal(size=n//2+1)
   for k in range(cutoff+1, n//2): # zero high; but also near high? Keep symmetric.
       zc[k]=0.0
   # ensure hermitian by ifft real? ifft(zc) maybe gives real if zc[-k]=conj(zc[k]); random not. We can just take real of ifft; it's a real sequence but frequencies not exactly symmetric? Actually real part of ifft of arbitrary complex is real but corresponds to some real sequence. Good.
   z=np.real(np.fft.ifft(zc))[:n]
   z=z-np.mean(z)
   # scale
   zstd=np.std(z)
   if zstd>0: z=z/zstd*0.5
   u=1.0+amp*np.tanh(z)
   return normalize_u(u,n)
```

```python
"""
Minimize 2*n*max(a*a)/(sum(a))^2 for nonnegative sequences.

This implementation works in the scale-invariant variable u = n*a (sum(u)=n),
minimizing max(u*u)/n.  It combines:

1. Blockwise L-BFGS-B optimization of piecewise-constant log-weights,
   with smooth max of the convolution coefficient peak.
2. A top-active-set KKT/Hankel least-squares flow.  For a selected active
   set of top convolution coefficients, the program repeatedly minimizes
   ||H_lambda u - r 1||^2 + nu||u - u0||^2 on the simplex.  Here
   (H_lambda u)_i = sum_j u_j lambda_{i+j}, so the flow pushes the
   active conjugate marginals toward the KKT stationarity condition.
3. Multiple lengths, previous-sequence starts, spectral/linear starts,
   and symmetric refinement.

The final sequence is selected by fast FFT scoring and then checked with
the supplied evaluate_sequence() when it exists.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False


DEFAULT_ACTIVE_PLANS = (
    (0.500, 0.30, 5),
    (0.350, 0.20, 6),
    (0.250, 0.12, 7),
    (0.180, 0.08, 8),
    (0.130, 0.05, 9),
    (0.090, 0.03, 10),
    (0.065, 0.02, 12),
    (0.050, 0.01, 14),
    (0.038, 0.005, 16),
    (0.028, 0.002, 18),
    (0.020, 0.001, 20),
    (0.014, 0.001, 22),
    (0.010, 0.001, 24),
)


def _next_pow2(m):
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def fft_size_for_n(n):
    return _next_pow2(max(1, 3 * int(n) - 2))


def project_simplex(v, target):
    """Project v onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=target} by bisection."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    target = float(target)
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(v.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)

    low = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    high = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(40):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        s = float(np.sum(np.maximum(v - mid, 0.0)))
        if s > target:
            low = mid
        else:
            high = mid

    tau = 0.5 * (low + high)
    q = np.maximum(v - tau, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        q = np.full(v.shape, target / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        q *= target / s
    return q.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def project_normalize(v, target, sym=False):
    """Project to simplex, optionally symmetrize and renormalize."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    q = project_simplex(v, target)
    if sym and v.size > 1:
        q = 0.5 * (q + q[::-1])
        s = float(np.sum(q))
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            q = q * (float(target) / s)
        else:
            q = np.full(v.shape, float(target) / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return q.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def normalize_u(v, target, sym=False):
    """Clip nonnegative, optional symmetrize, normalize sum to target."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    if sym and v.size > 1:
        v = 0.5 * (v + v[::-1])
    s = float(np.sum(v))
    target = float(target)
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(v.shape, target / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    v *= target / s
    return v.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def resize_u(old_u, n):
    """Interop/resample a sequence to length n and normalize sum to n."""
    n = int(n)
    old = np.asarray(old_u, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    old = np.where(np.isfinite(old), old, 0.0)
    old = np.maximum(old, 0.0)
    if old.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif old.size == 1:
        arr = np.full(n, float(old[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        x_old = np.arange(old.size, dtype=np.float64)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, old.size - 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = np.interp(x_new, x_old, old, left=0.0, right=0.0)
    return normalize_u(arr, n)


def score_from_b(b, n, sum_a):
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    sum_a = float(sum_a)
    if sum_a <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sum_a):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (sum_a * sum_a))


def convolution_u(u, S):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    n = u.size
    Lc = 2 * n - 1
    Fu = np.fft.rfft(u, S)
    b = np.fft.irfft(Fu * Fu, S)[:Lc]
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    return b, Fu


def score_u(u, S=None):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    n = u.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if S is None:
        S = fft_size_for_n(n)
    b, _ = convolution_u(u, S)
    return score_from_b(b, n, float(np.sum(u)))


def smooth_max_weights(b, beta):
    """Smooth maximum of b and softmax weights maximizing b."""
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(b.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0.0 or m <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(b.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        w *= 1.0 / float(b.size)
        return m, w

    x = beta * (b - m)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    z = float(np.sum(e))
    if z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(z):
        w = np.zeros(b.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        z = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / z
    G = m + float(np.log(z)) / beta
    return float(G), w.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def hankel_from_Fu(Fu, w, S, n):
    """(H_w x)_i = sum_j x_j w_{i+j} when Fu is rfft(x)."""
    wc = w.size
    need = n + wc - 1
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(np.ascontiguousarray(w[::-1]), S)
    C = np.fft.irfft(Fu * Fw, S)[:need]
    h = C[wc - n:wc][::-1]
    return h.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def hankel_pre(x, Fw, S, n):
    """Apply fixed H_w to x using precomputed Fw."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    wc = int((S - 1) + 1)  # not used directly; derive w length from n in outer.
    # w length is known implicitly as 2*n-1; but hankel_pre only receives Fw.
    need = 3 * n - 2
    Fx = np.fft.rfft(x, S)
    C = np.fft.irfft(Fx * Fw, S)[:need]
    # wc = 2*n-1
    wc = 2 * n - 1
    h = C[wc - n:wc][::-1]
    return h.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def smooth_grad_u(u, S, beta):
    """Smooth max of u*u and its gradient (modulo a constant)."""
    n = u.size
    b, Fu = convolution_u(u, S)
    G, w = smooth_max_weights(b, beta)
    h = hankel_from_Fu(Fu, w, S, n)
    g = 2.0 * h
    g = g - float(np.mean(g))
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return G, g, b


def add_candidate(candidates, u, n, S):
    u = normalize_u(u, n)
    sc = score_u(u, S)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        candidates.append((u, int(n), float(sc)))
    return candidates


def best_candidate(candidates):
    if not candidates:
        return None
    best = candidates[0]
    for cand in candidates[1:]:
        if cand[2] < best[2]:
            best = cand
    return best


def top_k_candidates(candidates, k):
    res = []
    for cand in candidates:
        inserted = False
        for i in range(len(res)):
            if cand[2] < res[i][2]:
                res.insert(i, cand)
                inserted = True
                break
        if not inserted:
            if len(res) < k:
                res.append(cand)
        while len(res) > k:
            res.pop()
    return res


def u_to_seq(u):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
    u = np.maximum(u, 0.0)
    u = np.minimum(u, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in u]


def official_score(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)  # noqa: F821
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if np.isfinite(val):
        return val
    return float("inf")


def uniform_u(n):
    return np.full(n, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)


def linear_u(n, a):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = min(0.95, max(-0.95, float(a)))
    u = 1.0 + a * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
    return normalize_u(u, n)


def spectral_u(n, rng, amp=0.8, cutoff=8):
    zc = rng.normal(size=n // 2 + 1) + 1j * rng.normal(size=n // 2 + 1)
    hi = max(0, n // 2 - max(1, int(cutoff)))
    if hi < zc.size:
        zc[hi:] = 0.0
    z = np.real(np.fft.ifft(zc))[:n]
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    sd = float(np.std(z))
    if sd > 1e-12:
        z = z / sd * 0.5
    u = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.tanh(z)
    return normalize_u(u, n)


def block_to_u_weights(c, n, B):
    n = int(n)
    B = int(B)
    L = n // B
    if L <= 0 or B <= 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.zeros(B, dtype=np.float64)
    if B > 1:
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        m = min(c.size, B - 1)
        if m > 0:
            c = np.where(np.isfinite(c[:m]), c[:m], 0.0)
            z[:m] = c
    if B > 1:
        z = z - float(np.max(z))
        w = np.exp(z)
        s = float(np.sum(w))
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            w = np.ones(B, dtype=np.float64) / float(B)
        else:
            w = w / s
    else:
        w = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    H = float(B) * w
    u = np.repeat(H, L)
    return u.astype(np.float64, copy=False), w.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def c_from_block_u(u, n, B):
    n = int(n)
    B = int(B)
    if B <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = n // B
    if L <= 0:
        return np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    h = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).reshape(B, L).mean(axis=1)
    h = np.maximum(h, 1e-8)
    c = np.log(h[:-1] / h[-1])
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return np.clip(c, -12.0, 12.0).astype(np.float64)


def block_objective(c, n, B, beta, S):
    n = int(n)
    B = int(B)
    u, w = block_to_u_weights(c, n, B)
    G, g, _ = smooth_grad_u(u, S, beta)
    L = n // B
    if G is None or not np.isfinite(G):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(max(0, B - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    gsum = g.reshape(B, L).sum(axis=1)
    wavg = float(np.dot(w, gsum))
    if B <= 1:
        grad = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        grad = float(B) * w[:-1] * (gsum[:-1] - wavg)
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return float(G), grad.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def run_block_minimize(c0, n, B, beta, bounds, S, max_iter=60):
    c0 = np.asarray(c0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    c0 = np.clip(c0, -12.0, 12.0)
    if B <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if c0.size != B - 1:
        tmp = np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(c0.size, B - 1)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = c0[:m]
        c0 = tmp
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return c0

    options = {
        "maxiter": int(max_iter),
        "ftol": 1e-10,
        "gtol": 1e-5,
        "maxls": 30,
        "maxcor": 10,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            block_objective,
            c0,
            args=(int(n), int(B), float(beta), S),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
        )
        c = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        c = c0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        c = c0
    return np.clip(c, -12.0, 12.0)


def block_initial_list(B, rng, n=None, prev_u=None):
    if B <= 1:
        return [np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)]

    m = B - 1
    inits = []
    inits.append(np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    for a in (-4.0, 4.0, -8.0, 8.0):
        inits.append((a * (x - 0.5)).astype(np.float64))

    half = m // 2
    c = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if half > 0:
        c[:half] = 3.0
    if m - half > 0:
        c[half:] = -3.0
    inits.append(c)

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, m, dtype=np.float64, endpoint=False)
    inits.append(2.0 * np.cos(t) * 1.0)
    inits.append(2.0 * np.sin(t) * 1.0)
    inits.append(1.5 * np.cos(2.0 * t))
    inits.append(1.5 * np.sin(2.0 * t))

    inits.append(1.0 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, m))
    inits.append(1.8 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, m))
    inits.append(0.0)  # placeholder for prev, added below if available

    if prev_u is not None and n is not None and int(n) % B == 0:
        c_prev = c_from_block_u(prev_u, n, B)
        inits[0] = c_prev

    out = []
    for c in inits:
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if c.size != m:
            tmp = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            mm = min(c.size, m)
            if mm > 0:
                tmp[:mm] = c[:mm]
            c = tmp
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
        out.append(np.clip(c, -12.0, 12.0))
    return out


def block_search_one_B(n, S, deadline, rng, B, best_u, best_sc, prev_u):
    n = int(n)
    B = int(B)
    local_best_u = np.asarray(best_u, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    local_best_sc = float(best_sc)

    if B <= 1:
        return local_best_u, local_best_sc

    if B <= 16:
        max_iter = 80
        max_starts = 10
    elif B <= 64:
        max_iter = 65
        max_starts = 8
    elif B <= 256:
        max_iter = 50
        max_starts = 6
    else:
        max_iter = 35
        max_starts = 4

    if prev_u is not None:
        inits = block_initial_list(B, rng, n=n, prev_u=prev_u)
    else:
        inits = block_initial_list(B, rng, n=n, prev_u=None)

    # Also include the current best projected to this block size.
    if best_u is not None and best_u.size == n:
        inits.insert(1, c_from_block_u(best_u, n, B))

    bounds = [(-10.0, 10.0)] * (B - 1)

    beta1 = 1.0
    beta2 = 4.0
    if B <= 64:
        beta1, beta2 = 0.7, 3.0
    elif B <= 256:
        beta1, beta2 = 0.9, 4.0

    best_c = np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    for idx, c0 in enumerate(inits[:max_starts]):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        c = run_block_minimize(c0, n, B, beta1, bounds, S, max_iter=max_iter)
        u, _ = block_to_u_weights(c, n, B)
        sc = score_u(u, S)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_sc:
            local_best_sc = sc
            local_best_u = u.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()

    # Sharper pass from the best coarse block solution.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        c = run_block_minimize(best_c, n, B, beta2, bounds, S, max_iter=max(20, max_iter // 2))
        u, _ = block_to_u_weights(c, n, B)
        sc = score_u(u, S)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_sc:
            local_best_sc = sc
            local_best_u = u.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()

    # If nothing improved, still keep best_c result to carry forward.
    u, _ = block_to_u_weights(best_c, n, B)
    sc = score_u(u, S)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_sc:
        local_best_sc = sc
        local_best_u = u.copy()

    return local_best_u, local_best_sc


def block_search(n, S, deadline, rng, max_B=1024, prev_u=None):
    n = int(n)
    best_u = uniform_u(n)
    best_sc = score_u(best_u, S)

    B_list = []
    for B in (4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096):
        if B <= max_B and B < n and (n % B) == 0:
            B_list.append(B)

    if not B_list:
        return best_u, best_sc

    if prev_u is None:
        prev_u = best_u

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    for idx, B in enumerate(B_list):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
        alloc = max(8.0, rem / float(max(1, len(B_list) - idx)) * 0.95)
        sub_deadline = min(float(deadline), time.monotonic() + alloc)
        best_u, best_sc = block_search_one_B(n, S, sub_deadline, rng, B, best_u, best_sc, prev_u)
        prev_u = best_u
        if idx % 2 == 0:
            print("block B={B} score={sc:.8f}".format(B=B, sc=best_sc), flush=True)

    return best_u, best_sc


def active_kkt_refine(u0, n, S, deadline, rng, sym=False, plans=None):
    if plans is None:
        plans = DEFAULT_ACTIVE_PLANS

    n = int(n)
    u = normalize_u(u0, n, sym)
    best_u = u.copy()
    best_sc = score_u(u, S)

    no_improve = 0
    Lc = 2 * n - 1
    nplans = len(plans)

    for idx, (frac, nu, inner_iters) in enumerate(plans):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
        alloc = max(6.0, rem / float(max(1, nplans - idx)) * 0.95)
        sub_deadline = min(float(deadline), time.monotonic() + alloc)
        frac = float(frac)
        nu = float(nu)
        inner_iters = int(inner_iters)

        while time.monotonic() < sub_deadline:
            b, _ = convolution_u(u, S)
            sum_u = float(np.sum(u))
            sc = score_from_b(b, n, sum_u)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                u = best_u.copy()
                sc = best_sc
                b, _ = convolution_u(u, S)
                sum_u = float(np.sum(u))
            elif sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc
                best_u = u.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1

            if no_improve > 80:
                p = float(rng.random())
                if p < 0.35:
                    u = project_normalize(u * (1.0 + 0.05 * rng.normal(size=n)), n, sym)
                elif p < 0.70:
                    u = project_normalize(u + 0.02 * rng.normal(size=n), n, sym)
                else:
                    u = best_u.copy()
                no_improve = 0

            if b.size == 0:
                break

            max_b = float(np.max(b))
            if not np.isfinite(max_b) or max_b <= 0.0:
                break

            K = int(round(frac * float(Lc)))
            if K < 1:
                K = 1
            if K > Lc:
                K = Lc

            if K >= Lc:
                idxs = np.arange(Lc, dtype=np.int64)
            else:
                idxs = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]

            w = np.zeros(Lc, dtype=np.float64)
            w[idxs] = 1.0 / float(K)
            weighted_avg = float(np.sum(b[idxs]) / float(K))
            r = weighted_avg / float(n)
            if not np.isfinite(r) or r <= 0.0:
                r = max(1e-6, max_b / float(n))

            wrev = np.ascontiguousarray(w[::-1])
            Fw = np.fft.rfft(wrev, S)

            z = project_normalize(u, n, sym)
            y = z.copy()
            t = 1.0
            alpha_base = 1.0 / (2.0 * (1.0 + nu))

            for _ in range(inner_iters):
                if time.monotonic() >= sub_deadline:
                    break

                h = hankel_pre(z, Fw, S, n)
                v = h - r
                hv = hankel_pre(v, Fw, S, n)
                grad = 2.0 * hv + 2.0 * nu * (z - u)
                grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
                grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)

                gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
                if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-14:
                    break

                step_norm = alpha_base * gnorm
                cap = 2.0 * float(np.sqrt(float(n)))
                if step_norm > cap:
                    alpha_eff = cap / gnorm
                else:
                    alpha_eff = alpha_base

                q = project_normalize(z - alpha_eff * grad, n, sym)

                rho = (t - 1.0) / (t + 1.0)
                y = q + rho * (q - z)
                y = project_normalize(y, n, sym)
                z = q
                t = 0.5 * (1.0 + float(np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t)))

            u = project_normalize(z, n, sym)

            b2, _ = convolution_u(u, S)
            sc2 = score_from_b(b2, n, float(np.sum(u)))
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc2
                best_u = u.copy()
                no_improve = 0

            if not np.isfinite(sc2) or sc2 > best_sc + 0.18:
                u = best_u.copy()

        # End of this active-set plan.  If the current path is much worse, reset.
        b_end, _ = convolution_u(u, S)
        sc_end = score_from_b(b_end, n, float(np.sum(u)))
        if (not np.isfinite(sc_end)) or (sc_end > best_sc + 0.10):
            u = best_u.copy()
        else:
            u = u.copy()

        if idx % 3 == 0:
            print("active plan {i} frac={f:.4f} score={s:.8f}".format(i=idx, f=frac, s=best_sc), flush=True)

    return best_u, best_sc


def choose_evaluation_candidates(candidates, prev_seq, max_count=3):
    top = top_k_candidates(candidates, max(max_count * 2, 4))
    chosen = []
    seen_lengths = []
    for cand in top:
        n = cand[1]
        if n not in seen_lengths:
            chosen.append(cand[0])
            seen_lengths.append(n)
            if len(chosen) >= max_count:
                break
    if prev_seq is not None:
        lst = [float(x) for x in prev_seq]
        if len(lst) > 0:
            arr = np.asarray(lst, dtype=np.float64)
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            if arr.size > 0:
                arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
                if float(arr.sum()) > 0.0:
                    chosen.append(arr)
    return chosen


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Return a nonnegative list of step heights with minimized evaluate_sequence score."""
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0
    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return [1.0]

    if budget_s > 1800.0:
        reserve = 260.0
    elif budget_s > 900.0:
        reserve = 150.0
    else:
        reserve = 60.0

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(30.0, budget_s - reserve)

    if budget_s < 300.0:
        N1 = 2048
        N2 = N1
        max_B1 = 256
        max_B2 = 128
    elif budget_s < 1000.0:
        N1 = 4096
        N2 = N1
        max_B1 = 512
        max_B2 = 256
    else:
        N1 = 8192
        N2 = 16384 if budget_s > 1500.0 else 8192
        max_B1 = 2048
        max_B2 = 1024

    N1 = int(N1)
    N2 = int(N2)
    S1 = fft_size_for_n(N1)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    candidates = []

    # Load previous state-of-the-art if available.
    prev_seq = None
    try:
        prev_obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if prev_obj is not None:
            lst = [float(x) for x in prev_obj]
            arr = np.asarray(lst, dtype=np.float64)
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            if arr.size > 0 and float(arr.sum()) > 0.0:
                prev_seq = arr
    except Exception:
        prev_seq = None

    prev_u1 = None
    if prev_seq is not None:
        prev_u1 = resize_u(prev_seq, N1)
        candidates = add_candidate(candidates, prev_u1, N1, S1)

    # ---- Length N1: parametric block search + multi-start active refinement
    T = max(1.0, float(deadline) - start_time)

    if N2 > N1:
        dl1 = min(deadline, start_time + 0.20 * T)
        dl2 = min(deadline, dl1 + 0.35 * T)
    else:
        dl1 = min(deadline, start_time + 0.25 * T)
        dl2 = min(deadline, dl1 + 0.65 * T)

    u_param1 = prev_u1 if prev_u1 is not None else uniform_u(N1)
    if HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < dl1 - 10.0:
        u_param1, _ = block_search(N1, S1, dl1, rng, max_B=max_B1, prev_u=prev_u1)
        candidates = add_candidate(candidates, u_param1, N1, S1)
    else:
        if prev_u1 is None:
            u_param1 = spectral_u(N1, rng, 0.8, 8)
        candidates = add_candidate(candidates, u_param1, N1, S1)

    if time.monotonic() < dl2 - 30.0:
        cur_best = best_candidate(candidates)
        if cur_best is None:
            cur_best_u = uniform_u(N1)
        else:
            cur_best_u = cur_best[0]

        refining_budget = max(0.0, float(dl2) - time.monotonic())
        if refining_budget > 20.0:
            start_items = []
            start_items.append((cur_best_u, 0.45, False))
            if prev_u1 is not None:
                start_items.append((prev_u1, 0.10, False))
            start_items.append((spectral_u(N1, rng, 0.7, 8), 0.10, False))
            start_items.append((spectral_u(N1, rng, 1.2, 5), 0.10, False))
            start_items.append((linear_u(N1, 0.55), 0.10, False))
            sym_u = project_normalize(0.5 * (cur_best_u + cur_best_u[::-1]), N1, True)
            start_items.append((sym_u, 0.15, True))

            total_frac = 0.0
            for _, fr, _ in start_items:
                total_frac += float(fr)
            if total_frac <= 0.0:
                total_frac = 1.0

            for u0, fr, use_sym in start_items:
                if time.monotonic() >= dl2 - 15.0:
                    break
                alloc = max(15.0, refining_budget * (float(fr) / total_frac))
                sub_deadline = min(dl2, time.monotonic() + alloc)
                ru, rs = active_kkt_refine(u0, N1, S1, sub_deadline, rng, sym=bool(use_sym))
                candidates = add_candidate(candidates, ru, N1, S1)

    # ---- Optional larger length N2: shorten, refine, and score
    if N2 > N1 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0:
        S2 = fft_size_for_n(N2)
        cur_best = best_candidate(candidates)
        if cur_best is None:
            base2 = uniform_u(N2)
        elif cur_best[1] == N2:
            base2 = resize_u(cur_best[0], N2)
        else:
            base2 = resize_u(cur_best[0], N2)

        rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
        if rem > 300.0:
            block2_alloc = min(0.22 * rem, 450.0)
            refine2_alloc = min(0.55 * rem, 1100.0)
        else:
            block2_alloc = 0.0
            refine2_alloc = max(30.0, rem - 120.0)

        u2 = base2
        if HAVE_SCIPY and block2_alloc > 60.0:
            dl_block2 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + block2_alloc)
            u2, _ = block_search(N2, S2, dl_block2, rng, max_B=max_B2, prev_u=base2)
            candidates = add_candidate(candidates, u2, N2, S2)

        if refine2_alloc > 40.0:
            tl = best_candidate(candidates)
            if tl is not None and tl[1] == N2:
                start_u2 = tl[0]
            else:
                start_u2 = u2

            start_items2 = []
            start_items2.append((start_u2, 0.55, False))
            start_items2.append((base2, 0.15, False))
            start_items2.append((spectral_u(N2, rng, 0.8, 8), 0.15, False))
            sym2 = project_normalize(0.5 * (start_u2 + start_u2[::-1]), N2, True)
            start_items2.append((sym2, 0.15, True))

            total_frac2 = 0.0
            for _, fr, _ in start_items2:
                total_frac2 += float(fr)
            if total_frac2 <= 0.0:
                total_frac2 = 1.0

            sub_total = refine2_alloc
            for u0, fr, use_sym in start_items2:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 60.0:
                    break
                alloc = max(15.0, sub_total * (float(fr) / total_frac2))
                sub_deadline = min(deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + alloc)
                if sub_deadline <= time.monotonic() + 5.0:
                    break
                ru, _ = active_kkt_refine(u0, N2, S2, sub_deadline, rng, sym=bool(use_sym))
                candidates = add_candidate(candidates, ru, N2, S2)

    if not candidates:
        return [1.0] * N1

    # Fast-score selection, then authoritative evaluation.
    top_candidates = top_k_candidates(candidates, 6)
    eval_seqs = []
    seen_lens = []
    for cand in top_candidates:
        n = cand[1]
        if n not in seen_lens:
            eval_seqs.append(u_to_seq(cand[0]))
            seen_lens.append(n)
        if len(eval_seqs) >= 2:
            break

    if prev_seq is not None:
        arr = np.asarray(prev_seq, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        if arr.size > 0 and float(arr.sum()) > 0.0:
            if arr.shape[0] not in seen_lens or len(eval_seqs) < 3:
                eval_seqs.append(u_to_seq(arr))
                seen_lens.append(arr.shape[0])

    # Collapse duplicates by length if many previous choices were added.
    seen = set()
    final_eval_seqs = []
    for seq in eval_seqs:
        ln = len(seq)
        if ln not in seen and 0 < ln <= 20000:
            final_eval_seqs.append(seq)
            seen.add(ln)
        if len(final_eval_seqs) >= 3:
            break

    best_seq = None
    best_off = float("inf")
    for seq in final_eval_seqs:
        off = official_score(seq)
        print("official score for length {ln}: {sc:.8f}".format(ln=len(seq), sc=off), flush=True)
        if np.isfinite(off) and off < best_off:
            best_off = off
            best_seq = seq

    if best_seq is None:
        # Fall back to the best by FFT score.
        bc = best_candidate(candidates)
        if bc is not None:
            best_seq = u_to_seq(bc[0])
        if prev_seq is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(prev_seq, dtype=np.float64)
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            if arr.size > 0 and float(arr.sum()) > 0.0:
                best_seq = u_to_seq(arr)

    if best_seq is None or len(best_seq) == 0:
        return [1.0] * N1

    # Final validation / clipping.
    arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        return [1.0] * N1

    # A scale-invariant rescale to a comfortable magnitude.
    if arr.size > 0:
        target_sum = float(max(1, min(arr.size, 8192)))
        arr = arr * (target_sum / s)

    out = [float(x) for x in arr]
    # One last official check if time is clearly available.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        off = official_score(out)
        if np.isfinite(off):
            print("final official score: {sc:.8f}".format(sc=off), flush=True)
        else:
            print("final official score invalid, fallback uniform", flush=True)
            return [1.0] * N1

    return out
```
